OFFICE OF THE CENTRAL PROVIDENT FUND 
COMMISSIONER 

New Delhi-llOOO'l, the 7th May 1987 

No. P.IV/1(4)/85.—^In exercise of the powers conferred 
by sub-section (7). of section 5D of the Employees’ Provi¬ 
dent Funds and Miscellaneous Provision's Act, 1953 (19 of 
1952), the Ctentrail Board, with the approval of the Central 
Government, hereby makes the following rules to amend the 
Employees’ Priovident Fund (Staff and Conditions of Ser¬ 
vices) Regulations, 1962 namely :— 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Employees’ Provident 

Fund (Staff and Conditions of Service) (Amend¬ 
ment) Regulations, 1987. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Employees’ -Provident Fund (Staff and Conditions 
of Service) Regulations, 1962, for regulation 33 the follow¬ 
ing shall be substituted namely:— 

“33 Saving: Nothing in these regulations shall affect re¬ 
servations, relaxation of age-limit and other 
, concessions required to be provided for the
